GCW — Card

WorldWide 100, 9 October 2008

9 October 2008
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
WorldWide 100 The O2 — London, England ---- Introduction The lights dim in the arena and the crowd begins to buzz. The GCW logo flashes across the screen, as "Wake Up" by Rage Against the Machine begins to play. Finally a heavy guitar riff explodes into the arena, and the London fans go wild! Bright blue fireworks explode over the ring as two screaming bolts of light stream down to the stage. The stage erupts with silver pyrotechnics as the cameras begin flying over the frenzied crowd, and the voice of James Bryan chimes in right on cue. Bryan: Welcome everyone to the biggest episode of WorldWide of all time! Tonight, GCW's flagship broadcast celebrates its one hundredth birthday with the biggest free broadcast in GCW history! Hello, everyone, I'm James Bryan coming to you for the hundredth time, alongside David Yale for the, well, eightieth or something, from swinging London! Yale: Thanks, JB, and I may not have been there for the first one, but I'm damn well here for this one, and this is the most important WorldWide yet! This is going to be an absolutely massive night, made all the bigger by the fact that it's just one week before Dangerous Games on Pay-Per-View! Bryan: That's right, in just over a week thirty wrestlers will step into that ring to compete for a shot at the GCW World Heavyweight Championship at NC-17! But before we can get there, we've got a massive lineup for you tonight! Yale: And we're going to start by looking right at the main event, JB! Jay Terror defends his GCW Title against Hall of Famer Jason Dread, who signed with the company just for this match! There's no telling what he'll do if he wins, but it really doesn't matter! Tonight, Jay Terror will prove how great he is by defeating a Hall of Fame member! Bryan: This represents Terror's biggest challenge since winning the World Title, and really his only challenge! But a win tonight will give him huge momentum heading in to his huge Pay-Per-View showdown with Jordan White, which was just signed this week! Yale: And speaking of momentum, Jordan White's got tons of it, but he runs right into a man with a bit of momentum himself, the unbeaten Garbage Bag Johnny! Something's got to give here tonight. Will Jordan White lose all his momentum before his World Title shot, or will Johnny's streak finally end? Bryan: We've also got Vivica J. Valentine teaming up with former friend and adversary Tessa Windsor to take on this odd team of Chris Bagwell and Clyde Fox. Bagwell's got Fox under his spell, and that would seem to give him a huge advantage over the dysfunctional tandem of Valentine and Windsor! Yale: Well, it's a chance for Tessa to upstage Vivica and further get out of her shadow, so I think you'll see Tessa's a-game tonight! Bryan: In other action, Digital Mortality defends the US title against Zachariah Taylor, in what is a huge opportunity for that young man! Yale: Yeah, but Digi's been a sensational United States Champion, JB. That's a clash of two really superb wrestlers, and only one can leave with the belt! Bryan: Also the Tag Titles are on the line, the TV Title is up for grabs in Mr. Danger's return to TV, and a lot more! But perhaps the biggest match of the night is the incredible "I Quit" match between LeStatt Knight and Andy Murray. Yale: Two of the greatest stars in GCW today are meeting, JB, and they've got incredible respect for one another. But that's going to be a brutal match, and it's got huge implications going in to Dangerous Games! Bryan: That's right, Knight and Murray are both on a quest to win that match, and the winner tonight will clearly have the upper hand heading in to next week's Pay-Per-View. But Murray and Knight are both on top of their games, and an I Quit match with LeStatt Knight is always a dangerous proposition. Yale: Yeah, those two simply have to hope to survive long enough to even make it to the show, JB! Bryan: This is going to be an historic night here on X:TV, so let's get things started here on WorldWide 100!!! ---- Reminiscing About the Future "Established" cues up right away, and the crowd greets with with more than their usual level of contempt. Golden fireworks begin sparking to life in the rafters as two frantic aids rush down the ramp, unrolling a red carpet over the steel. Following shortly is President Steven Caldera, beaming proudly despite the overpowering boos. Bryan: And it looks like WorldWide 100 is being kicked off in the only way it could, with a visit from GCW President and Owner Steven Caldera! Yale: Who else could possibly lead off this historic broadcast, JB? This is the man that took this company from nothing to worldwide proportions, and he didn't just do it once, he did it twice! Bryan: Well, the fans love to boo this man, but you suspect that they're boos of respect. You've got to give the man credit, he's done some wonderful things for GCW, even if his personal skills leave something to be desired. But he's got a lot at stake with his Establishment in the coming weeks, and I have a feeling he's got a lot to say on the subject. Caldera struts down the red carpet, swinging a GCW microphone in his hand as he reaches the ring. He brushes the two ring aids out of his way before crossing to the steps, where he climbs smoothly into the ring. Andrews: Please welcome the owner and President of Global Championship Wrestling, Steven Caldera! The boos return with renewed vigor as Caldera waves politely to the crowd. His music quickly fades and he slowly turns in the center of the ring, taking a long look at the crowd. Bryan: Caldera's soaking it all in... Yale: This is an emotional moment for him, JB! I don't think this means as much to anyone as it does to Caldera. After a moment, Caldera takes a deep breath and begins to speak, despite the fact that the crowd reaction has yet to subside. Caldera: One hundred episodes. Let me just repeat that: one hundred episodes. You know, Meltdown ran for about sixty episodes, a little over a year. I thought that was quite an achievement in an industry where wrestling promotions come and go overnight. Caldera's frank words seem to quiet the crowd, though they seem ready to explode again if given a reason. Caldera: GCW returned in 2005, mostly out of boredom. We'd all gone our separate ways after the company closed in '02. I spent my time commentating and working behind the scenes here and there, but it was always painfully obvious to me that I needed to be the man in charge. Because I'm the best in the world at what I do, and I knew I was depriving the wrestling fans of the world of the quality product they deserved. I was depriving them of Global Championship Wrestling. The crowd tries to boo this, but can't quite bring itself to. Caldera: WorldWide debuted in February of 2005. Here we are now, October of 2008, and WorldWide is still going strong here at episode one hundred. And tonight's show is a celebration of all the stars, all the great moments, and yes, even you, the fans, who have made this show the premier wrestling broadcast in the world. Warming up to Caldera's sentimentality, a smattering of cheers comes down from the crowd. Bryan: Wow, Caldera sounds positively magnanimous, Dave. Yale: Hey, this moment transcends everything that's gone on lately in GCW, JB. This is about history. Caldera: But you know something... Caldera grins, flashing fierce eyes at the camera. Caldera: ...looking back at the past three years, the past ninety-nine episodes of WorldWide, I realize... I've made some mistakes. This positively stuns the crowd, nearly silencing them. Caldera: I realize that I haven't always done things right, that I haven't always acted in GCW's best interests. And the company has suffered at times because of my... weakness. And for that, I apologize. Bryan: Umm... wow? Yale: I... I'm not sure what to think just now, JB. Caldera: Because there have been times when I've catered to you people, when I've buckled to the wrestlers in back, when I've let lesser men call the shots. Because until recently, WorldWide has been Establishment-free, and for that, I owe every single one of you an apology. This company needs me, it needs The Establishment, it needs men like Tyson DeBough, Octavian and Jay Terror who know what it takes to lead a worldwide wrestling promotion. So WorldWide 100 is not just a celebration of the greatness of GCW, but a celebration of the fact that WorldWide is finally where it needs to be, it finally has the champion it deserves, and I am finally leading the way as only I can! A monstrous chorus of boos pours down at the conclusion of Caldera's heated statement. A snarling grin comes across his lips as he chuckles at the gullible crowd. Bryan: Yeah, should have seen that coming. Yale: Phew. For a minute there I thought Caldera had been replaced by his evil twin! Caldera: Because you need us, GCW. And what's more, you know you need us. Global Championship Wrestling and The Establishment go hand in hand. You can't have one without the other and expect this company to be the best it can be. You need us because you need someone to hate. Because you know we're the best at what we do, you know I'm always going to be here, pulling the strings. And your delicate sentimentality tells you that you're not supposed to like people like that, you're supposed to rally behind the "nice" ones. We give you people a reason to show up every week, we give all those white-collar nice guys a purpose they wouldn't have otherwise. And I, I am the one that makes it all tick, that keeps this place on fire. And so I submit to you, GCW, indisputable proof that WorldWide 100 is as great as it is because of Steven Caldera and The Establishment. The boos continue, rattling the roof as Caldera closes his eyes to absorb all they've got. Caldera: Tonight that truth will be ironclad. Tonight, the Heavyweight Champion of the World Jay Terror takes on a Hall of Famer, Jason Dread. He- Caldera is cut off by the sound of an electrifying response for Dread. Bryan: This crowd is excited to see Dread back in action in GCW! In tonight's main event he's got a chance to become a three-time World Champion! Caldera: He's going to step into the ring with a true legend, a true Hall of Fame talent. And he's going to win. He's not just going to win, though, he's going to put on a performance, he's going to give you people an example of what life would be like if Jordan White weren't lurking about a title he doesn't deserve. Tonight is going to prove to everyone once and for all that Jordan White is out of his league, and after Dangerous Games, there won't be any denying it. The crowd boos hard as Caldera pauses. He draws the microphone and opens his mouth only to be drowned in the intro strains of "Sweet Cream In It." The crowd's exuberance rolls again as Jordan White swaggers out from beneath the MegaTron, head swiveled up to watch a newly added clip of himself snapping into Octavian with a superkick like lightspeed. He smirks and he nods; the president of GCW rolls his eyes and shakes his head. Bryan: And, right on cue, the man Jay Terror has finally signed on to face at Dangerous Games--if he manages to make it out of WorldWide 100 with that belt! Yale: I just can't believe that even Jordan's spiteful enough to interrupt Caldera tonight. If he was ever going to show a hint of respect, a little shred of decency, he'd still be in his locker room right now. Bryan: Respect? After what he's been through at the hands of the Establishment, I'm amazed he's not running down the ramp to get a shot at Caldera right now. Yale: With how socially repugnant he is, I can't believe it myself. Jordan runs a hand back through unkempt hair and swings a mic by the cord to his side. He returns the stare Steven Caldera fires at him from the ring. The crowd gets tired or decides to give them time to talk; they quiet to a near silence, broken by the occasional whooping and the more frequent single scream. White: Don't that act ever get old? You know, the whole "I'm gonna act like a decent person for once, then laugh my ass off when I reveal I'm still a giant fuckin' prick" routine? Steven Caldera furrows his brow, lowers his eyes; he shakes his head again. White: Well, damn. Guess there's just a divide somewhere 'tween us, huh? Makes sense, but, hell. I didn't decide to ruin your big moment so's to discuss how me an' you are different people. Caldera: I really wish you'd get on to whatever you did come out here for. White: An' that's why I'm takin' my time, boss. I don't wanna be the only person's gotta deal with a runaround. Jordan White grins, it's returned with a sneer from the ring. White: See, here's the thing, Stevesy. Your boy, Terror, this little idiotic spree he's been on? Man, you can't tell me with a straight face that that's what you'd call a serious title reign or whatever. All he's done since takin' the belt is piss me off--an' maybe that's fine by you, but I don't think many of the people tunin' in every week, drivin' however many hours to catch us live, are doin' that to watch Jay Terror sit on his ass and talk shit. The audience goes explosive again; it seems Jordan White's onto something. White: See what I mean? These people showed up 'cause they wanna see Jason Dread walkin' out of here the world champion an' Jay Terror not walkin' out of here. They can't get the time he's wasted back, man, but they'll damned sure be happy to see it repaid in some serious carnage. He smiles teeth and lets the mic hang as the jubilation in attendance runs ripples down the aisles. White: And that's the deal. Terror's made his ass mighty hard to find now that he's finally got a match, so I've gotta level this to you. If ol' boy keeps his little golden trinket tonight, manages to find the bravado or whatever it's gonna take to survive Jason Dread? When he gets to Dangerous Games, I want to introduce him to somethin' brand new to his little career: I wanna introduce him to ACWF. And that's it; whatever the crowd had been holding back is unleashed in waves, in a wall of sound that buries commentary, conversation, contemplation. They want gore, and if Jay Terror makes it past Dread, they want him on the pyre. Bryan: ACWF! OH MY GOD! Yale: Is he insane?! Bryan: Jordan White is calling for an ACWF match with Jay Terror! Caldera: ACWF? Are you sure about that, Jordan? I mean, the last time you were in an ACWF match, it didn't exactly work out for you. And now you want to step back in between the chains with the deadliest wrestler alive today? White: Only after I took the Ironman from him; remember that, 'cause I do, an' I know exactly what I'm gettin' myself into here. I'm okay with it, I'm cool with the potential repercussions of this one: I'm puttin' myself on the line here 'cause I want the chance to take more from the kid than his belt. Bryan: For all the aimlessness and apathy that he's been accused of before, I don't think there's any mistaking Jordan White's intent now. Ever since Jay Terror turned his back on him for a shot at wrestling's highest honor, He's momentarily drowned out by the human noise. Bryan: --and you can't blame him if he wants to take his toll in blood! Yale: You really dislike our world champion. Bryan: You're damned right I do. Caldera paces a tight square at the center of the ring; his gaze is on the floor, his hand to his chin. He cuts a chuckle short and raises his head. Caldera: Fine! Ask and ye shall receive. Jordan White, Jay Terror, GCW World Title... ACWF Match at Dangerous Games! The crowd roars its enthusiasm for the idea. Bryan: It's on! My God, what a blockbuster! Caldera: But Jordan, be warned: this is your last shot. This is your last hurrah. Up until now I've tolerated you in the spotlight, but now the spotlight is starting to stink. I want to wash your stench off this company, and at Dangerous Games, Jay Terror is going to do that for me. Jordan White nods. White: Good. Tell him to be ready. The mic clatters to the steel flooring, rattles over the P.A. as Jordan turns. Caldera: Enjoy your night... I hope you're ready for what's coming. And the number one contender disappears through the curtain without another word. Caldera steps out of the ring and adjusts his tie as the camera returns to the announce crew. Bryan: WOW! What a shocker! Jordan White wasn't just content with his title shot, he wanted to make it an ACWF match! Yale: Well, that's the most brutal, unholy of all matches, JB, and I think Jordan might have just signed his death warrant. I applaud his newfound enthusiasm, but he's clearly lost his mind! Bryan: Fans, Dangerous Games just got even bigger! Jay Terror defends his World Title against Jordan White in an ACWF match! What a start to this night! ---- WW1 Flashback: Welcome to WorldWide The GCW logo flashes briefly on the screen, before quickly fading out. The shot opens into a wide view of a frenzied crowd. Every last one of the seventeen thousand people in Nationwide Arena are on their feet, literally leaping in enthusiasm. The arena lights are dimmed, save for a single spotlight in the center of the ring. Standing there, clad in a perfectly pressed new tuxedo and looking fit and young, James Bryan stands with a microphone in his hand. From beneath a thick brown goatee he smiles, his eyes darting across the crowd as the camera zooms in for a closer look. Bryan- Ladies and gentlemen… Welcome to Global Championship Wrestling. He states the words slowly and methodically, but the crowd explodes nonetheless. With another sly grin, Bryan continues. Bryan- Thanks to each and every one of you who are here tonight, and to each and every one of you watching at home. With your support, Global Championship Wrestling will once again become the greatest wrestling promotion there is. So, without further ado, Global Championship Wrestling presents… WORLDWIDE!!! Bryan’s last words bring an enormous explosion from the rafters, as blue and silver pyro begins pouring down from the ceiling! ‘Wake Up’ by Rage Against the Machine begins booming in as the lights in the arena come up, and a string of blue flames runs down from the rafters to the stage, colliding with it to create an incredible blast of fire rising in front of the MegaTron. The fireworks finally subside, as the cameras begin panning rapidly over the crowd, displaying each and every fan to be going wild with anticipation. James Bryan’s voice returns overhead from the broadcast booth, sounding much more enthusiastic this time. Bryan- Welcome back, everybody! After nearly two long years, Global Championship Wrestling has returned! Hello, everyone, I’m James Bryan, back with you again at the broadcast booth here at ringside! And it’s my distinct pleasure to introduce to you my new broadcast colleague, and the PRESIDENT of Global Championship Wrestling, Steven Caldera! The crowd’s incredible reaction continues as Wake Up blasts into the foreground again, and the cameras focus on the stage. The new stage is large and raised from the ground, with an enormous steel version of the GCW logo framing the MegaTron above the entrance. A moment passes before a tall, fit figure emerges onto the wide, long stage. Clad in a fine Italian suit and a broad smile, Steven Caldera opens his arms in welcome to the crowd. The roar is massive for Caldera as he heads for the ring, slapping hands with the fans on his way. After just a few long strides he reaches the ring and slides inside, where he is promptly handed a microphone by ring announcer Joey Andrews. Caldera nods in appreciation as the music subsides overhead. Caldera turns in a full circle, taking in the spectacle, as cameras flash over the ring to capture the momentous occasion. Caldera slowly observes the ring itself, then places a hand on the top rope and gazes upward to the bright lights shining down on the ring. After a few moments Caldera raises the microphone to his mouth and begins to speak. Caldera- I’ve always known that promoting professional wrestling is my calling, my purpose for being. But these past few weeks have brought a wealth of doubts to my mind. I found myself, as we grew closer and closer to this moment, wondering if I still had it in me. If I was still capable of running an efficient and entertaining wrestling promotion. And with that doubt came a lot of reflection, realizing how much I’d changed in the past few years and wondering how it would effect me. But now, standing beneath these lights again, on the biggest stage in the world of professional wrestling, there’s no doubt about it. Steven Caldera and Global Championship Wrestling are back! The roar is incredible in response to Caldera’s words, as every fan in the arena jumps to their feet again to applaud the owner. Caldera- Before I came out here tonight, I planned on promising to run this company right, swearing never to manipulate things to my advantage again. But the moment I stepped through those ropes, something dawned on me. I’m the owner of this company, and I have the right to do whatever the hell I want! The crowd, still supportive of Caldera, erupts once again. Caldera- Now, that doesn’t mean I’m not a little older and a little wiser. I’m not the same stubborn son of a bitch you all used to love to hate. But I do feel that I know better than anyone how to handle matters out here, so let it be known that my authority is absolute, and there is absolutely nobody back there that can circumvent it. That said, I promised everyone an announcement, and here it is. In regards to the GCW World Heavyweight Championship, we have a new title belt, and a new belt deserves a new champion. In case anyone out there hasn’t heard yet, we’ll be crowning that champion in six weeks, in a six man ACWF match! Another loud explosion from the crowd, anxiously awaiting the legendary match. Caldera- Now, with six open spots for that match, it leads to a very interesting question. Who gets in? Who gets the shot? Well, I pondered this for a long time and considered all the ways we could decide six contenders to the title. But in the end, I decided that there’s only one guy qualified enough to decide the participants for that match… me! So the task before each and every member of the GCW roster is this… Impress me. I want everyone to wrestle their hearts out. Because every match counts, every win and every loss matters. The six guys that impress me the most are the guys that will have the privilege of competing in ACWF Six for the GCW World Heavyweight Championship! As the crowd applauds again, the thin strains of Might of Rome begin creeping into Nationwide Arena. The cheers immediately sour as a magnificent form appears on stage. Caldera’s head snaps around with a knowing grin as a familiar face begins heading for the ring. Octavian, former 3-time GCW champion, is wearing a magnificent gold-chain male vest over a solid black silk shirt, black pants and a menacing scowl. Clearly more muscular than the last time he was seen, Octavian appears to be in superb condition, and walks smoothly to the ring, ignoring the boos from the crowd. With a light step he climbs the steel steps with a dignified air, steps through the ropes, and produces a microphone of his own from his back pocket. Caldera meets eyes with Octavian with a firm stare, as Octavian begins to speak in his Roman accent. Octavian- It’s- Octavian hardly begins before his voice is drowned out by the jeers of the crowd. The fans are on their feet expressing their disgust with the Italian Stallion, but Octavian remains patient. After a moment the boos subside, and he presses on. Octavian- It’s good to be back in this ring with you again, Steven. I can’t tell you how good it makes me feel. Because for the past two years I have been waiting very patiently for this day to come. Most people never thought they’d see GCW again, but most people don’t know you like I do. I know you better than you do, Steven, and I always knew you’d bring GCW back from the dead. I knew your ego wouldn’t allow you to stay out of the spotlight forever. And now, as always, I am here to remind you that your power is not absolute. That as long as you exist, I will be there to fight against you. You and I are opposites, Steven, and we will always be in conflict. So don’t be so arrogant as to think I won’t do everything in my power to take this company for you. Your purpose in life is to promote wrestling. Mine is to take it from you. The crowd boos, and Caldera is observably affected by this. But his voice remains strong as he responds. Caldera- I had a feeling you would be the first to come out here, Gaius. I just knew you wouldn’t be able to wait your turn to get your voice heard. So I’ll tell you what I’m going to do. Rather than fight you, or argue with you, or try to stop you, I’m just going to sit back and listen to you. Then, when you’re done, you’re going to turn your Roman ass around and walk straight back to your locker room, and wait there until I tell you to come out. The crowd cheers in response as Caldera simply backs into the corner, tucks his microphone under his arm, and looks at Octavian in impatience. Octavian- Fine then, I’ll tell you what I want. I want what’s mine. I want the GCW World Heavyweight Championship back! The crowd boos at this predictable answer, but Octavian ignores them. Octavian- You see, when I heard you say that you were giving six men a shot at that title, it occurred to me that this simply isn’t fair. It isn’t fair because of everyone back there, Steven, nobody has the credentials to carry that title that I do. I’m a three time World Champion, Steven… The ONLY three time champion! I held the title for seventy three days! I successfully defended that title more times than anyone else! And, most importantly, Steven… The last time I won that title, I had it TAKEN from me by you! I never lost the GCW World Title, Steven, it was stolen from me. And now I order you to give it back! The crowd’s boos drown out all sound in the arena, as Caldera contemplates his next move. Before he can respond, however, a new sound fills Nationwide. Just Got Wicked begins booming overhead, and all eyes turn back to the stage. Clad in blue jeans and no shirt is Jason Dread, who is received with a mixed reaction from the crowd. Dread ignores the fans reaching out to touch him and slides right into the ring, accepting another microphone from Joey Andrews. Dread takes a position directly between Caldera and Octavian, glancing at both of them. His music fades as he finally turns his back to Caldera, facing directly at Octavian. Dread- You know, I never realized until just now how good it feels to be standing in front of a GCW crowd… The fans explode in appreciation, though Dread’s focus remains locked on Octavian, who studies him curiously, a little upset at being interrupted. Dread- And to subject them to that kind of bullsh*t isn’t something I’m prepared to do. Greatest champion of all time, eh, Octy? Let’s think this one through a bit. When did you get your big break? When you became leader of the Establishment. Oddly enough, I got to the top as leader of the Establishment, too. You held the title for three months… I held the title for three months. Sure, you beat me by a few days, but the fact is my reign will always be listed right next to yours as greatest ever. But there’s a difference between you and I, Octy. You were handed the GCW Title by the man behind me. Me? I worked from the bottom up to get there. I had to wrestle with Dan Rivero for two months before getting a shot at the spotlight. You say you’re Caldera’s opposite? Well then I’m yours. Because we’ve done the same things in this company, Octy, but I’ve done them all by myself. Are you really mad at him for taking away your title? How can you be mad at him for taking something away from you that he gave to you in the first place?! The crowd bursts out in cheers as Octavian glares angrily at Dread, taking a step forward. Caldera instantly springs forward to stop any potential outburst, stepping between the two. Octavian sighs and steps back a few feet, his eyes burning a hole in Dread. Octavian- You? You could never have been if it wasn’t for me, Jason Dread. I created the Establishment. I created your identity. And you stole it from me. Considering the fact that you simply followed the trail I blazed, I should think you ought to show a bit more respect to the man you owe your life to. Dread, angered by the remark, tries to push forward but is restrained by Caldera. Dread- I’m sick and tired of having to listen to you talk about how you’re the franchise of this company. Tell you what. You want to prove yourself? Earn your way into the ACWF match. If you want to prove that you’re really better than me, that you’re the original, then earn that shot into the ACWF match and win the title for yourself. I guarantee I’ll be seeing you there. Octavian- I have already earned the right to compete for that title as the greatest champion of all time. And you, Steven, are not the only man capable of deciding who deserves to be there. I- Octavian’s voice is immediately drowned out by a familiar drum beat. The heavy tones of My Own Summer flood Nationwide Arena, and the Columbus crowd reacts with the biggest explosion of the night! A few moments pass before The Jhub comes jogging out on stage! The explosion is deafening as cameras begin to flash again, bathing The Jhub as he basks in the glow of the arena. The three men in the ring all retreat to a neutral corner as The Jhub heads for the ring, smiling all the way down. He slides into the ring and perches himself on the remaining turnbuckle, throwing his arms up in the air to the delight of the crowd. Bryan- Ladies and gentlemen, this is a shocker! I had no idea The Jhub was back with GCW, but here he is before our very eyes! This crowd is going absolutely wild, and this situation may start to get out of hand! The Jhub hops back down and expertly produces a microphone from his belt. Caldera smiles, apparently having been in on The Jhub’s presence, but Dread and Octavian stare in amazement as The Jhub observes the ring’s occupants. Jhub- Octavian… ya got your Green Card yet?? The crowd erupts in appreciation as Octavian simmers in anger. Jhub- And Jason Dread… last time I saw you, you were lying on your back, staring up at the lights as yours truly left with his new world title belt! Dread shakes his head in disbelief as the crowd applauds again. Jhub- And Steven… you're still a choade...but I’m damn glad to be back! But how could you disrespect me by calling yourself the authority on ACWF? After all, everyone knows The Jhub is the Mohammad, Moses, Jesus, Vishnu, and just flat out GOD of the ACWF! More applause from the crowd, as The Jhub smiles back at the camera. Jhub- You see, I was doing just fine sipping martinis and getting massages from blonde Swedish women all day long, but when I heard those four letters, I knew it was time to come back. After all, cold hard pavement and a nice powerful forklift beat a pair of double d’s any day, right? The crowd chuckles in disagreement, but continue to applaud The Jhub. Jhub- Ok, ok, I'm full of it. There's nothing better than a nice pair of tig-ass-bitties. The crown erupts in agreement. Jhub- So I’ve got to say, guys, before either one of you start tearing each other apart for the title of best champion ever, maybe you should consider what you’re getting into. Because I’m here tonight to tell you all that The Jhub will be in ACWF Six, and any man stepping through the chains is going to find himself screaming to get out by the time I’m done with them! Caldera- Well, it seems we’ve got a bit of a problem here. Just because you three think you deserve the shot doesn’t mean I’m just going to hand it to you. Camera fades to black. ---- Nusery Rhymes with Oblivion Shaman sits in a chair in the middle of the boiler room as he smiles and lifts a book. Shaman: As I was going to sell my eggs, I met a man with bandy legs, Bandy legs and crooked toes; I tripped up his heels, And he fell on his nose. Shaman just simply turns the page and looks up at the camera after he does so. Shaman: Hell no meaning to that one i just liked the violent outcome. Reminds me of how i am going to be tossing idiots out on their faces. Shaman turns the page to the next rhyme and he simply stares at it. Shaman: This one has an interesting story behind it and i am going to place it within the walls of the GCW with an explanation and comparison. Shaman chuckles and begins reading. Shaman: It is called Mary Mary quite contrary! Listen closely because you shall be tested. Shaman taps his fingers as Mayhem looks over his shoulder at the book. Shaman: Mary, Mary quite contrary, How does your garden grow? With silver bells and cockle shells And pretty maids all in a row. Shaman grins as he looks up. Shaman lemme explain this a bit for you. I want you to understand what is at stake here. Shaman stands up and walks to a chalk board as he turns it over. He grabs a pointer stick and smacks the chalk board. Shaman: Because it is an English nursery rhyme, the most commonly accepted historical interpretation is that the rhyme refers to Mary Tudor (Mary I of England) also known as Bloody Mary. Mary was the daughter of Henry the VIII and his first wife Catherine of Aragon. Her young life was full of upheaval and at one point, she was demoted from Princess to simply being ‘Lady Mary’ when her father separated with the Roman Catholic Church, became head of the Church of England, and demoted Catherine from her role as Queen, which in turn made Mary illegitimate. However, she did ultimately become Queen. Mary’s first move, as queen, was to retroactively legalize the marriage between Henry and her mother and thus legitimize herself. Her second move was to find herself a Catholic husband so that she could provide herself with an heir and thus remove any opportunity for her Protestant sister Elizabeth from ascending the throne if she were to die. Therefore, at the age of 37, Mary married a prince from Spain named Philip who later became Philip II. Eleven years her junior, Philip did not find Mary desirable; the marriage was simply a political move for him. Mary never succeeded in providing an heir for them. She had two false pregnancies and ultimately died from what appears to have been ovarian cancer.A staunch Catholic, Mary tried to reverse the damage done by her father’s break with Rome. In her attempts to rid her country of Protestantism and restore Catholicism, Mary persecuted and murdered many Protestants. Mary’s personal history and the nature of Catholicism lead to several distinct interpretations of the rhyme. Shaman clears his throat and moves on to the next section. Shaman: One interpretation suggests that silver bells were Catholic cathedral bells, that the cockle shells were the symbol of pilgrimage to the Catholic Shrine of St James in Spain, and the pretty maids all in a row were Catholic nuns. A second interpretation views the cockle shells as a lewd comment on her relationship with her disinterested husband, Philip II of Spain. The question How does your garden grow? was a scornful attack of her inability to produce an heir. And the pretty maids all in a row was a reference to her miscarriages. A final interpretation goes to the heart of her persona as “Bloody Mary” with silver bells and cockle shells referring to instruments of torture: silver bells being thumbscrews and cockles shells being instruments of torture attached to the genitals. While the pretty maids would be a reference to early guillotine-type devices used to decapitate victims. Shaman grins and pushes the chalk board out of the way as he turns and sits back down in his chair laughing. Shaman: I do so love that last part. Thumb screws and instruments of torture attached to the genitals. Imagine the excruciating pain if say it was attached to someone like Bagwell or Clyde Fox. It would be like not having to listen to Brittany Spears sing. Truth is i want to do some personal GCW comparisons here. Shaman leans back in the chair propping on arm behind it. Shaman: You see the GCW Champion is like Mary Tudor always lobbyign and jockeying to make things their way. Yet always behind the scenes there are political powers at work. Zenith and Caldera who are working to make this there own and control it. The pretty maids are the matches we are in to gain the position to take something away. We all act like Guillotines, but whether we are sharp or dull depends on the skills and if we defeat our opponents. The torture devices are what you see here before you. Shaman waves his hand towards himself and then to Mayhem standing beside him. Shaman: We are torture devices called Oblivion. Shaman lifts the book one last time and runs his hand down the page. Shaman: Oh yes this one with a classic twist with my own words put in place. Please do not scream for it is for your own safety and understanding. Parents i would advise yous end your children out of the room. It has been known to cause children to have nightmares and grown ups to simply piss themselves. But Dread, calm and collected, keeps his palms on the mat, locking down even harder on the move. Unable to move to the side, Terror surprises Dread by rocking straight up, managing to plant his extended leg on the mat. Barely able to keep his balance, Terror stands over Dread, but Dread keeps the hold locked in! Bryan: Wow, Terror’s managed to stand, but still Dread won’t release it! Yale: But he’s putting pressure on Dread’s shoulders, he’s got him pinned! Terror leans forward, forcing Dread into the canvas, and Briggs makes the count! …1! …2! …Dread shoots an arm into the air to kick out, but Terror grabs it and holds on. Dread tries to fire a punch with his other arm, but Terror grabs that one, too. Terror then rocks back, pulling Dread up while dropping back to the mat. He then kicks ferociously with his legs, severing the move and flipping Dread over the top rope and all the way down to the floor. Bryan: WHAT A COUNTER! Oh my God, Terror almost had Dread pinned, but he just reversed Dread clear out of the ring! Yale: Have you ever seen a figure four countered like that before, JB!? That was brilliant! Bryan: I’ll give the son of a bitch credit, that was an amazing reversal, and not a moment too soon! You can tell the damage was done, Terror’s knee has got to be screaming in pain, but Dread might have had the wind knocked out of him when he landed on the floor. Dread lifts his head but nothing else, unable to move. Terror scrambles to the ropes to pull himself up, but puts no weight whatsoever on his injured left leg. He grits his teeth and tries to shake the pain off, watching intently over the horizon of the mat for Dread to reappear. Bryan: Both men are really hurt here, in the middle of what has been an incredibly violent World Title matchup! And you said it earlier, Dave, Terror has to be thinking ahead to Jordan White and wondering if Jason Dread is dealing out more damage than Terror can afford to be taking! Yale: Forget about all that now, JB. Terror needs to just focus on the task at hand, or there won’t BE any match with Jordan White! Bryan: Getting worried now? Yale: NO! But Jay’s wounded, and he needs to finish this thing off fast! Dread’s head finally pops up over the apron, and Terror tries to step forward to cut him off. But Terror’s knee fails him and he drops to the mat, allowing Dread to roll freely into the ring. Both wrestlers stand and meet in the center of the ring, with Terror sticking a woozy Dread with a hard right. Dread manages to fire back, and Terror loses his balance and staggers back into the corner. Bryan: Terror’s doing all he can to keep on top of Dread, but he just can’t put any weight whatsoever on that leg! Yale: It’s ok, I don’t think Dread’s got much right now, that fall really took a lot out of him! Terror tossed him about fifteen feet in the air! Bryan: That was a devastating fall, but Dread’s still brawling! Gasping for air, Dread rushes into the corner, but Terror manages to slip out of the way. Dread hits the turnbuckle and Terror throws a big forearm into his face. Terror begins pummeling Dread, firing off hard forearms and elbows into Dread’s face. A large cut opens above Dread’s right eye, pouring blood down his face; the sight of the bright red liquid only inspires Terror to hit harder and faster, hammering Dread down to the mat. Seeing Dread in trouble, the official once again steps in to try to slow things down, but Terror shoves him away again and continues the assault. Bryan: God, Terror is absolutely wearing Dread out here! Yale: He’s snapped! He’s had about enough of this nonsense, he’s going to make Dread pay for daring to take this match! Terror finally breaks off the attack when his knee buckles, but he catches himself in the ropes and keeps his feet. The crowd explodes in boos as he stares out at stands, wiping Dread’s blood off of his arm with his jeans. He then picks Dread up and locks him up from behind! He tries to pull Dread up, but Dread reaches out and grabs the top rope. Bryan: And now Terror’s going for it all! He’s got Dread in position for the Bandstand Bust, but Dread won’t go over! Yale: He’s holding on for dear life here, JB! Seeing Dread in the ropes, Briggs walks over and grabs Terror in an attempt to break the lock. Terror refuses, still trying to pry Dread away. Finally Terror’s grip is loosened and he rears back, drilling Briggs in the face with his elbow! The crowd boos as Terror snarls at the official before turning back to continue his attack. But Dread slips behind him and rolls him up with a schoolboy! Briggs dives into position… …1-2-3! Briggs’ hand hits the mat three times in rapid succession, and the crowd explodes! The bell sounds and Dread rolls out of the ring, throwing his arms into the air. Bryan: OH MY GOD! DREAD GOT HIM! DREAD’S THE CHAMPION! Yale: WHAT?! NO NO NO!!! Bryan: Jason Dread rolled Terror up, and Briggs made the count! We’ve got a new World Champion! “Just Got Wicked” cues up again, and the roar grows even higher. Briggs rolls out of the ring and raises Dread’s arm in the air, leaving a stunned and bewildered Terror standing with wide eyes in the ring. Briggs is handed the title belt, which he presents to a beaming Dread to hoist into the air. Bryan: We’ve got a new World Champion! I don’t believe it! What a stunning finish to this match! Yale: There’s no way that was legit, and you know it! That was a fast count! Bryan: It might have been a little faster than usual, but Terror hit the official one too many times, Dave! What do you expect?! Yale: I expect him to be a professional and do his damn job! This is highway robbery!!! Terror begins screaming at Dread below him as Dread places the belt over his shoulder and begins backing up the ramp. The crowd continues its incredible roar as Dread taunts Terror, who kicks the turnbuckle in fury. Bryan: And Terror is losing his mind! Jason Dread has shocked the world and become a three-time GCW Champion!!! Yale: No no no no!!! Before Dread can get any further up the ramp, a voice calls out, penetrating his booming music and the thunderous crowd. “Hold on just a damn minute!” Dread’s music abruptly cuts out, and President Steven Caldera steps out on stage. Following close in tow is a confused looking Cameron Wrigley. Caldera: Hold on just a damn minute! No, no, no, there is no way this match is ending like that! A deafening chorus of boos greets the President as he glares down at Dread, who nervously adjusts the belt on his shoulder. Bryan: Oh, hold on a minute… Yale: Yes! Finally, some law and order! Caldera: Just what the hell is going on here? Do you people really think Jason Dread is walking out of here like a thief? No, this match is going to continue! Bryan: Well, that figures… Yale: He’s doing the right thing, JB! Briggs counted Terror fast, and this match should still be going! Caldera: And I want YOU— Caldera points accusingly at Briggs down at the ring. Caldera: --out of the damn ring! I’ll deal with you later. And you— Caldera glares at a steaming Dread. Caldera: --You drop that belt and get your ass back in the ring. Now. Dread angrily tosses the belt in Caldera’s direction, and Wrigley walks down the ramp. Briggs lowers his head, rubs his hurt jaw, and begins walking up the ramp. Bryan: Wow! For a moment there it looked like Jason Dread had taken the title, but Caldera’s ruling it a fast count and wants this match to continue! Yale: Come on, even you have to admit it was unfair, JB. Bryan: Fine, but Terror just abused Josh Briggs during this entire match, and Briggs got a little carried away. But this match is going to continue, and Dread still has a chance to win this thing fair and square! Wrigley and Dread each step into the ring, while a breathless Terror tries to regain his composure in the corner. The bell sounds again, and the match resumes! Bryan: And here we go! Round two! Yale: Come on, Jay, quit screwing around! Limping hard but finally able to use his wounded leg, Terror circles Dread before the two tie up. The crowd roars back as Dread slips behind Terror and into a rear waistlock. Terror rushes forward and drops, sending Dread rolling off his back and into the corner. The two return to their feet and Terror steps forward, only to walk into a straight kick to the face. Bryan: BOOM! This match has continued, and it’s Dread with the huge move! Terror’s down and in trouble! Yale: This place is going absolutely crazy, JB! I’m not sure I’ve ever seen this before in a GCW Title match! Bryan: Well, we’re going to get a fair finish either way, I suppose! Stunned, Terror looks up at Dread through glassy eyes and gets back to his feet on instinct alone. Dread begins peppering him with right hands, backing him into the center of the ring. Dread then backs into the ropes to get his momentum up, charging Terror and going for a cobra clutch on the way. But Terror sidesteps him and hooks him around the neck from behind! Bryan: Oh no! Dread was going for the Tres Flores, but Terror countered! Yale: BANDSTAND BUST! Terror lifts Dread into the air and pushes him forward. But Dread grabs Terror’s arm and drops down behind him, landing flat on the canvas and drilling Terror with a massive slam! Bryan: COUNTER! TRES FLORES! Yale: He got it! Bryan: Dread countered into the Tres Flores! What a sensational move! He’s got him beat! Wrigley drops to make his first count as Dread hooks a leg, and the crowd chants along! …1!!! …2!!!! …And at the last moment, Terror kicks out! Bryan: NO! Oh, so close! Dread leveled Terror with the Tres Flores, that big cobra clutch slam, but Terror somehow kicked out! Yale: YES! God, that was a close call! I thought it was over, but Terror escaped with his title reign still intact! Shocked, Dread turns and slips out of the ring, heading up to the top rope. The few seated members of the crowd rise as Terror crawls up. Dread leaps off the top rope but Terror springs into action, meeting Dread with a dropkick to the chest! Dread collapses to the mat, and suddenly Terror catches a second wind. Bryan: Oh what a move! Dread came down with a big high-risk move, but Terror made him pay! And now it’s Terror with the advantage! Yale: This is just going back and forth now, JB! That might have been the mistake Terror needed to finish this match off! Struggling to breathe, Dread instinctively gets off his back as Terror hits the ropes. Terror jumps, but Dread catches him and launches him higher into the air! Dread drops Terror hard onto his face, then climbs onto his back. Bryan: COUNTER! Dread’s on top again! He crushed Terror with that big pancake, and now he’s… He’s going for the No Moment! Yale: Oh God, get out of there, Jay! Bryan: Terror’s got no clue what’s about to happen! He’s never seen anything like this before! Dread hooks Terror’s legs into his own and pulls back on his arms. He then rocks back, suspending Terror in the air. A shocked Terror screams in pain as Dread rocks him in the air, putting as much pressure on as possible. Bryan: NO MOMENT! He’s got it locked in! Terror’s got nowhere to go! Yale: God, no, someone, Caldera, anyone! Bryan: There’s no way out of this hold! Dread got him locked down! There’s nowhere for Terror to go! It’s over! Yale: There’s got to be something he can do! Damnit, don’t give up, Jay! Dread cinches in harder, arcing Terror’s back to a sickening degree. Wrigley checks in but Terror refuses to submit, even as he begins to black out. But with one sudden burst of energy, Terror frees his legs and flips high into the air, still suspended by Dread! Shocked, Dread lets go, and Terror curls straight down, driving a knee directly into Dread’s face! Bryan: WHAT A COUNTER! Terror somehow broke that hold and came down with a kneedrop to the head! I’ve never seen that before! Yale: GENIUS! That’s what I’m talking about, JB! That’s why this man is the greatest! The amazed crowd buzzes wildly as Terror scrambles away from Dread, holding his back, his eyes twisted with pain. Clutching his still-bleeding face, Dread rises and runs straight at Terror. Terror sees him coming but can’t do anything to avoid the contact as Dread wildly flings himself into the corner. He crushes Terror against the turnbuckle. Bryan: Terror broke free, but Dread’s right back on him! He’s not going to give Terror any time to recuperate! He was in the No Moment a long time, I don’t think he’ll be able to continue here! Yale: There’s got to be a way… Dread turns and wraps an arm around Terror’s head, rushing out of the corner for a bulldog. He leaps, but Terror keeps running, throwing Dread off and through the ropes! Dread flies spread-eagled into the steel post, earning a pained “oh!” from the crowd. Terror immediately wraps Dread up and pulls him out of the corner, spinning him around into the ring. Bryan: BANDSTAND BUST! Here it comes! The crowd explodes as Terror pushes Dread out and drops him square on his head. Terror grabs at his back in excruciating pain, but finally rolls over above Dread. Bryan: HE GOT IT! Terror hit the Bandstand Bust! Yale: FINALLY! Dread had escaped it all night, but Terror finally got him! It’s over! Wrigley drops in position for the count, but Terror doesn’t go for the pin. Instead, his face twisted with rage, he lifts Dread back up and hooks him from behind again. Bryan: Oh God, wait a minute! What the hell is he doing?! Yale: He’s going to do it again! Terror lifts Dread up and sits down even harder this time, rattling the canvas with the impact. The crowd roars with boos, and this time Terror hooks the leg. Bryan: A second Bandstand Bust! God, Terror wanted to make Dread pay! Here’s the cover! …1! …2! …3!!! Bryan: THERE IT IS! Yale: YES! TERROR RETAINS! The crowd explodes as “No Apologies” hits again. Terror sits up on the mat, obviously in great pain, unable to move. Wrigley raises his arm, but Terror angrily snatches it back down, still favoring his back. Andrews: Here is your winner, and STILL the GCW World Heavyweight Champion… JAY TERROR! Bryan: Damnit, the son of a bitch did it! Terror hits Dread with two Bandstand Busts and wins this match! Yale: God, that was close! My heart almost stopped when Dread was awarded the belt, JB, but Caldera came out and did the right thing! Bryan: Well, for a moment it looked like Dread might have shocked the world in his GCW return, but things didn’t go his way! But for a man that hasn’t been in a professional wrestling ring in three years, this was an astonishing performance! He took the fight to Jay Terror and damn near pulled off a third title win! Yale: I’ll give the man credit, JB, but Terror’s just too good! Maybe now we can finally set aside this ridiculous debate and give Jay Terror the credit he deserves as a great champion! Bryan: This was a tremendous win for him, Dave, no doubt about it. But he took a lot of punishment tonight, and now he’s got to turn right around and fight Jordan White in an ACWF match! Neither one of those guys might make it out alive! Terror finally inhales and wills himself to his feet. Wrigley hands him his GCW Title belt, which he snatches away greedily and replaces over his shoulder. He stares down at Dread, who doesn’t move after the two massive shots to the head. Bryan: And there it is! Jay Terror walks out of WorldWide 100 with an impressive victory over a GCW legend! But he’s got the toughest challenge of his life ahead of him! Yale: Well, Jason Dread won the biggest ACWF match of all time, JB, so I think this gives Terror some momentum! Bryan: Tonight belongs to Jay Terror, no doubt! Jordan White loses, Terror beats Dread, things are looking up for The Establishment! But when Terror walks out here at Dangerous Games next week and finds himself in between the Asphalt, Chains, Wrenches and Forklifts, will he have what it takes to retain the GCW Title? Ladies and gentlemen, we’re out of time from WorldWide 100! What a night it’s been, and it only gets bigger in one week at Dangerous Games from right here in London! For David Yale, I’m James Bryan, saying same time, same place next week, live on Pay-Per-View!!!!!! WorldWide comes to a close as Jay Terror raises the GCW Title belt over his head, standing tall over Dread’s motionless body, ready for the biggest challenge he’s ever faced……
